# Phase31 release check

**Date:** 2026-10-29
**Branch:** `arena/48320ee5-ilmerastudyabroad`
**Baseline:** Phase30 commit `75f355fd4dfda0a61ddbda5b45fc2944c8503e76`
**Disposition:** Phase31b verification and reporting are complete for the requested local checks. No confirmed source defect was found, so no site fix was made; no public site source changed during Phase31b. Task4 local route-budget failures remain explicit and carry to Phase32. This report is not a live-site, native-host, deployment or speed certification.

## Result by gate

| Check | Result | Evidence and interpretation |
|---|---|---|
| Task5 full verification suite | PASS | 31/31 groups passed, no skips or resume; PHP-WASM 8.5.10 lint covered 98 PHP files. |
| Discovery and strict wording | PASS | Discovery, strict discovery routes, wording, citation and editorial/policy checks passed. |
| Palette and visual policy | PASS | Visual policy on, off and inline checks passed. |
| Structured data and publication policy | PASS | Strategy schema, publication and source policy checks passed; rendered JSON-LD matched on all 43 routes. |
| Sitemap and technical SEO | PASS | 38 sitemap entries and exact sitemap bytes matched the Phase30 baseline; canonical, robots, status and technical SEO checks passed. |
| 43-route rendered-content comparison | PASS | Text/order, title/meta, headings, FAQs, links, image attributes, JSON-LD, canonicals, robots, HTTP statuses and sitemap invariants passed. |
| Source-scope guard | PASS | Only `index.php`, `about.php` and `includes/visual-system.php` were authorized Phase31 public presentation sources. |
| Browser matrix | PASS | 306/306 cases: 258 normal-motion and 48 reduced-motion representative cases across 43 routes, with no horizontal overflow, no hidden rendered page text and all 732 image instances loaded. |
| Axe WCAG A/AA | PASS, baseline-relative | Axe-core 4.11.0 on eight representative routes at 1280px, JavaScript on and reduced motion: `/`, `/services`, `/about`, `/contact`, `/study-in-germany`, `/study-abroad-counselling-mysuru`, the selected article and `/blog/`. Existing 36 violation nodes remained; no identities were added or removed. This is not a zero-violation certification. |
| Interactive target geometry | PASS, unchanged | All 5,670 baseline/current width-height pairs match exactly at captured precision. The 2,840 observations with a 44px height remained 44px. This is a baseline comparison, not a new sitewide minimum-size certification. |
| Fresh blank-space scan | PASS | 516/516 cases; exact scan source and render index are `49efa0a`. Four Contact cue tails remain intentionally retained. |
| Before/after contact sheet | PASS | 32 full-page raw PNGs, 8 pages x 2 widths x 2 source arms, all HTTP 200 and all returned to top. |
| Task4 per-route local A/B budget | FAIL, carried to Phase32 | Only 2/8 routes passed every local budget. `performance_release_certified=false`. This is a single local simulation, not live or field evidence. |
| Live speed, field data and native hosting | NOT RUN | Not part of this Phase31b local verification. No live speed claim is made. |
| Phase31 ZIP build | SKIPPED BY DESIGN | Phase32 owns the final current-branch ZIP and completeness/hash verification. |
| Merge or deployment | NOT RUN | Nothing was merged or deployed. |

## Render and reference-page comparison

The comparison passed all **43 route entries**. It was captured at `f05a7d3559db9dbd2ad3512076d1c480f0d8a4de` against the baseline `75f355fd4dfda0a61ddbda5b45fc2944c8503e76`. The rendered source files used for the fresh 516-case scan and contact sheet are at `49efa0aa1e929c9359bc6e2abc3ba656d8e19753`; there are no public PHP/CSS/asset differences between the Task5 render source and that scan source.

Protected invariants passed: all rendered text and order; title and meta attributes; headings; FAQs; link hrefs, attributes, order and multiplicity; image src/alt/dimensions/loading/srcset and attributes; JSON-LD graphs; canonicals; robots directives; HTTP statuses; exact sitemap bytes and 38 entries. The source-scope guard passed for `about.php`, `includes/visual-system.php` and `index.php`. No standalone file named Phase31 fix log was present; approved spacing and visual deltas are recorded in the Phase31 progress log and the Task2/Task3 scope reports.

| A/B reference/control route | Difference between Task4 arms |
|---|---|
| `/` Home | Byte-identical rendered HTML; no still CSS. |
| `/services` | Byte-identical rendered HTML; no still CSS. |
| `/about` | Byte-identical rendered HTML; no still CSS. |
| `/contact` | Byte-identical rendered HTML; no still CSS. |

The four A/B targets (`/study-in-germany`, `/study-abroad-counselling-mysuru`, the selected article and `/blog/`) differ only by the single logged Task3 static-still CSS block. Removing that block makes each B rendered page byte-identical to A. No content, metadata, image content or other style changes entered the A/B candidate. The two still assets are byte-identical between arms.

## Blank-space scan and height accounting

The fresh scan completed **516/516** cases across 43 routes x 3 widths (360, 768, 1280) x JavaScript on/off x normal/reduced motion. Thresholds were 120px, 160px and 200px respectively. The frozen route index and scan source head both match `49efa0aa1e929c9359bc6e2abc3ba656d8e19753`.

Audit observations: **1,006** pixel quiet-band candidates, **4** explicit min-height tails, and **16** intentional cue dismissals. Hidden reveal/page-copy failures: **0**. Non-decorative empty wrappers: **0**. Pixel quiet-band findings are screening candidates, not approved savings.

**Observed full-document height reduction: 505px** across the three affected responsive views: Home at 360px (-58px), About at 360px (-180px), and About at 768px (-267px). The 768px document-height delta is from the fresh full scan, not the contact-sheet image set.

The separate pre-fix visible-child-to-section-bottom `#team` tail measurements are 201px for Home at 360px, 196px for About at 360px and 283px for About at 768px, summing to 680px. That pre-fix section-tail metric is not the same as whole-document height change. Repeated JavaScript/motion observations are not added. Contact `#miniHero` tails of 138px at 360px and 233px at 768px are retained for the existing bottom scroll cue and are excluded from savings.

The fresh post-Task3 scan has fewer quiet-band candidates than the Task1 scan (1,174 to 1,006). Still backgrounds affect pixel texture; that delta is not counted as spacing removed.

## Task4 local simulated A/B results

One frozen session recorded 112 samples (56 per arm, seven per arm per route), all with valid sample status and no retry/resume. The median-LCP control rule passed 4/4 reference routes, but this is a median-only local control check. Route budget results below remain the deciding local result. The full sample is simulated through local fixtures and pinned resources; do not claim live speed or field performance.

| Route | B/A median LCP | Local failure | Route budget |
|---|---:|---|---|
| `/` | 0.9955 | CLS 0.000779 > 0.000769 | FAIL |
| `/services` | 0.9748 | TBT +115ms > 50ms; CLS 0.003891 > 0.000000 | FAIL |
| `/about` | 0.9842 | CLS 0.006346 > 0.004126 | FAIL |
| `/contact` | 0.9607 | None | PASS |
| `/study-in-germany` | 1.2233 | LCP ratio > 1.10 | FAIL |
| `/study-abroad-counselling-mysuru` | 1.0657 | None | PASS |
| `/blog/how-to-choose-study-abroad-consultant-karnataka` | 1.1366 | LCP ratio > 1.10 | FAIL |
| `/blog/` | 1.1362 | LCP ratio > 1.10 | FAIL |

**A/B disposition:** `performance_release_certified=false`. The six route failures are Phase32 findings, not a Phase31b live-speed verdict. Do not rerun Task4 or describe these values as live-site speed.

## ZIP and release boundaries

No ZIP was built during Phase31b, by design. Phase32 must build the final archive from the then-current branch and verify its completeness and hashes before packaging is considered complete. Historical Phase30 archives are not represented as a current Phase31 package. Native Apache/LiteSpeed behavior, live font/CDN delivery, field performance, contact delivery, backup/restore and deployment were not tested here. No merge or deployment occurred.
